AssistantMessage(content=[TextBlock(text='Done. `./gradlew clean assembleDebug` → **BUILD SUCCESSFUL**, and the full unit test suite passes.\n\n## Root cause\n\n`DocumentIO.saveDocument()` decided whether to touch the disk by comparing the editor text against a **history snapshot** instead of the actual file:\n\n```java\nif (!currentText.equals(documentInitial.getContent())) { ...write... } else { ret = true; }\n```\n\nFor a new note, undo/redo history is disabled by default (`isEditorHistoryEnabled()` → `false`), so `DocumentEditFragment.loadDocument()`\'s `addToHistory()` is a no-op and the history stays **empty**. Then inside `saveDocument()`:\n\n1. `document.setDoHistory(true)` re-enables history,\n2. `document.setFile(<title>.md)` triggers `addToHistory()`, which snapshots the document **as it is right now**,\n3. `getInitialVersion()` returns that snapshot as `_history.get(0)`.\n\nIf the user waited ≥ `HISTORY_DELTA` (5 s) — precisely when the save icon becomes enabled — the delayed `_document.setContent(text)` had already pushed the typed text into the model, so that first snapshot *already contains the typed text*. The comparison is therefore equal, the write is skipped, and `saveDocument()` still returns `true`. The back press (`onPause` → `saveDocument()`) hits the same condition, so **the file is never created**. Skipping the wait leaves the snapshot empty, which is exactly why that path worked.\n\n## Fix\n\n1. **`DocumentIO.saveDocument()`** — base the write decision on the real filesystem state: write if the target file doesn\'t exist, or if its on-disk content differs from the editor text. This also fixes the related silent-data-loss case where editing content back to an earlier history state was never persisted.\n2. **`DocumentEditFragment.saveDocument()`** — after a save, sync the fragment arguments to the now-real file (`EXTRA_PATH` + `EXTRA_PATH_IS_FOLDER=false`). Previously the arguments still said "this is a folder", so any `onResume()` → `checkReloadDisk()` would build a brand-new empty document with a fresh random filename and discard the note just written.\n\n## Verification\n\nAdded `app/src/test/java/net/gsantner/markor/util/DocumentIoSaveRepoTest.java` replicating the reported steps plus regression guards.
